      CALL MP_ML5_0_TERMINAL_WL_0_0(WL(1,0,1,2639),4,COEFS,1,4,WL(1,0
     $ ,1,2685),WL_ENTRY_INEXACT)
      CALL MP_ML5_0_CREATE_LOOP_COEFS(WL(1,0,1,2685),0,4,744,1,1,3677
     $ ,H)
C     Coefficient construction for loop diagram with ID 1696
      CALL MP_VVS1L3P0_1(PL(0,2639),W(104),GC_85,MDL_MW,MDL_WW,PL(0
     $ ,2686),COEFS)
      CALL IEEE_GET_FLAG(IEEE_INEXACT,WL_ENTRY_INEXACT)
      CALL MP_ML5_0_TERMINAL_WL_0_0(WL(1,0,1,2639),4,COEFS,1,4,WL(1,0
     $ ,1,2686),WL_ENTRY_INEXACT)
      CALL MP_ML5_0_CREATE_LOOP_COEFS(WL(1,0,1,2686),0,4,744,1,1,3678
     $ ,H)
C     Coefficient construction for loop diagram with ID 1697
      CALL MP_VVV1L2P0_3(PL(0,2680),W(6),GC_4,MDL_MW,MDL_WW,PL(0,2687)
     $ ,COEFS)
      CALL MP_ML5_0_UPDATE_WL_0_1(WL(1,0,1,2680),4,COEFS,4,4,WL(1,0,1
     $ ,2687))

      GOTO 1001
 4000 CONTINUE
      MP_LOOP_REQ_SO_DONE=.TRUE.
 1001 CONTINUE
      END
